Title: Discorsi della vita, et attioni di Carlo Borromeo, prete cardinale di Santa Chiesa del titolo di Santa Prassede, arcivescovo di Milano
Creator: Giovanni Battista Possevino, Mantuan ("Di Giouan Battista Posseuino Mantouano" on the title page); dedicated to Cesare Speciano, bishop of Cremona, formerly Borromeo's agent at Rome
Publisher: Appresso Iacomo Tornieri (the dedication places the author at the court of Rome; the place of printing is not legible in the OCR title page)
Publication Date: 1591
Rights: Public Domain (published 1591; public domain by date)
Language: ita
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_ZsccrEWFV4IC, file bub_gb_ZsccrEWFV4IC_djvu.txt, fetched directly by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session), 2026-10-16; host rights statement: licenseurl http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/ (Public Domain Mark), no possible-copyright-status field, access-restricted-item not set; contributor National Central Library of Rome (Google Books scan). A second scan of the same edition is on archive.org as bub_gb_RgCw2Hp2_V8C with a noisier OCR.
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged (long s count 0, so no long-s normalisation; nothing retyped, nothing dropped, nothing corrected). A 1591 print, so public domain by date.
Content note: An early Life in discourses by a member of Borromeo's own household (the dedication says the author served the cardinal for many months and was called to his deathbed), arranged by topic ("Cap." headings, with an index of chapters at the end). OCR sampled at 10/30/50/70/90 %: legible, but the long s is read as f throughout and some words are broken; quotations must be verified against the page images before use. No English translation exists. Not to be confused with the Jesuit Antonio Possevino.
---
